title: Count the Islands
difficulty: Medium
position: 7
complexity: O(r * c)
=== statement
A map is a grid of land `#` and water `.`. Land cells that touch up, down, left or right belong to the same island. Count the islands.

One island can wind through the whole grid, so deep recursion may run out of stack.
=== input
The first line has the number of rows `r` and columns `c`. Then `r` lines of `c` characters follow.
=== output
Print the number of islands.
=== constraints
1 <= r, c <= 512
=== sample
4 5
##...
##..#
....#
.#.##
--- expected
3
=== sample
3 3
...
...
...
--- expected
0
=== test
1 1
.
--- expected
0
=== test
1 1
#
--- expected
1
=== test
2 2
#.
.#
--- expected
2
=== test
3 3
###
#.#
###
--- expected
1
=== test
